# address-inventory.md Precompile and agent-function address inventory, per-address methods, events and errors, error-registry rows and activation gates at the source revision

> This inventory was generated by the specification rebuild. The normative, machine-readable address table and error-code registry that the code builds and tests against (services/agent/crates/layerx-types/build.rs, scripts/ci/result-codes-gen.py, precompiles/common/lxp_errors_test.go, cmd/layerxctl/tests/files.rs, the wallet-gateway and SDK tests) is [address-table.md](address-table.md); its sections (a), (a2), (b) and (c) keep their layout under change control. Interface and task references with anchors that exist only here point to this file; line references point into address-table.md.

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Version | 2.0.0 |
| Source revision | 2a1033aa2 |
| Change control | changes only by amendment; each amendment raises the version of this file and of README.md |
| Generated from | the merged interface blocks of the specification (kinds precompile_method, precompile_event, precompile_error of domains 2, 3, 4, 6, 13, and every interface or acceptance block whose contract names this file) |
| Owning contracts of ABI detail | ledger-auth.md (0x101A, 0x101C), pay.md (0x101B), programs.md (0x101D), agent-functions.md (0x0901-0x0908), markets.md (0x1008, 0x1015, 0x1020-0x1025), launchpad.md (0x1017), web.md (0x1019); this table is the address-level index and freezes the code-owned ABIs of the other addresses |

Status values: stable (code and intent agree at the source revision, no task changes it), change (served at the source revision, behaviour or shape changes), new (absent at the source revision; the covering task creates it), retire (served at the source revision, the covering task removes or refuses it). Selectors are the values recorded in the interface blocks: first four bytes of keccak-256 of the canonical signature for methods, topic0 for events.

Scope totals: 1455 precompile interfaces (312 precompile_error, 245 precompile_event, 898 precompile_method); by status 992 stable, 314 change, 85 new, 64 retire.

### Unserved retirements
The superseded table promised that 0x1012, 0x1013 and 0x1014 refuse their retired methods with Error("LXP_MODULE_DISABLED: <name> retired"). The helpers RetiredAt and RetiredRevert exist at precompiles/common/retire.go:24 and :34, but precompiles/layerxverify, precompiles/layerxcustody and precompiles/layerxanchor call neither at 2a1033aa2. Interfaces of status retire at these addresses and their covering tasks:

| Address | Retire interfaces | Covering tasks |
|---|---|---|
| 0x1012 | 5 | 6.71.1 |
| 0x1013 | 26 | 6.45.1 |
| 0x1014 | 33 | 6.32.1, 6.32.2 |

### Superseded gateway route-map constants
The superseded table listed gateway constants 0x...0800 (staking), 0x...0400 (bech32) and 0x...0100 (p256) that are not chain addresses. No TypeScript file under services/, apps/ or sdk/ carries the 0x...0800 or 0x...0400 constant at 2a1033aa2; no row remains to fix.

## (b) Error code registry (D6)
Kernel codes are the lxp_result X-macro list (include/layerx/lxp_result.h:29-157). The Go revert registry precompiles/common/lxp_errors.go:40-313 is the source of codes and retryability for precompile reverts; a precompile reverts with Error(string) carrying "LXP_<CODE>: <detail>". Rows are taken from the phase-1 events-and-errors inventory.
